# Experiment ledger (SLURM accounting, Isambard-AI)

> **Compute cost for the paper appendix: do NOT read it off this file.**
> Rows below are written at SUBMIT time, so their `Elapsed`/`State` columns are frozen at
> `PENDING / 00:00:00`. The real cost (queue wait, wall time, allocated CPUs/GPUs, peak RSS)
> comes from SLURM accounting:
>
> ```
> python make_cost_table.py --since 2026-07-01     # conda env lobs5 (py3.10)
> ```
> writes `5_analysis/results/cost/{cost_by_step.md, cost_by_step.tex, cost_jobs.csv}` —
> the `.tex` is a ready booktabs table for the appendix, the `.csv` is one row per job and
> doubles as a snapshot in case accounting is purged.


Auto-generated by `update_experiment_ledger.py` on 2026-07-24 15:10 (jobs since 2026-06-30). Regenerate after every fleet. All jobs are one node; AllocCPUS is the node allocation (72 = full node), GPUs are H100.

- **2329 jobs** (1502 COMPLETED), wall-clock sums over all states.
- **GPU-hours: 3280** total / 2924 in completed jobs; **CPU-core-hours: 277942**.
- Queue wait = Start − Submit. n_samples/fleet notes are hand-maintained in the script.

## 2026-07-27 04:20 — cost audit: 47% of a day's compute was burned by timeouts

Measured over the 24 h to 2026-07-27T04:00 (249 jobs, ~36,557 CPU-hours):

| | jobs | CPU-hours | share of waste |
|---|---|---|---|
| **timed out, produced only their headline stage** | 22 | **17,136** | 100% |
| — of which the beta pipeline (`beta`, `beta12`, `betaOWQR`, `lobimp_a5_beta`) | 17 | 14,688 | 86% |
| — `pubfig` | 2 | 864 | 5% |
| — `figrest`, `lbb_N_Hawkes`, `lobimp_master` | 3 | 1,584 | 9% |

Root cause of the 86%: `run_beta.sh` ran three stages after the headline that are labelled
"diagnostic only" in their own banners and take no `--stock`, so every per-stock job recomputed all
stocks. Five concurrent per-stock jobs therefore did the same all-stock work five times over and all
five hit the 12 h wall inside `beta_master_curve.py` — every time, on every stock, never once
finishing. Fixed the same day: `run_beta.sh` stops after the headline stage by default (`FULL=1`
opts back in, and the diagnostic pass should be submitted once, not once per stock).

Consequence while it lasted: `EffectvUsage` 0.614 against `NormShares` 0.048 drove FairShare to
0.088, every job to `Priority=1`, and the queue to a standstill — a 1-core/2 GB/5-minute probe job
(5791366) sat pending behind other users just like the 96 GB ones. The starvation was self-inflicted
and roughly half of it bought nothing.
